function Toggle({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: boolean;
  onChange: (v: boolean) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={value}
      onClick={() => onChange(!value)}
      className="flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-frost/15 bg-frost/5 px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm hover:border-frost/30"
    >
      <span className={value ? "font-medium text-foreground" : "text-muted-foreground"}>
        {label}
      </span>
      <span
        className={`relative h-5 w-9 shrink-0 rounded-full transition-colors ${
          value ? "bg-pulse" : "bg-frost/15"
        }`}
      >
        <span
          className={`absolute top-0.5 size-4 rounded-full bg-frost transition-all ${
            value ? "left-[1.15rem]" : "left-0.5"
          }`}
        />
      </span>
    </button>
  );
}

function Gauge({ index, level }: { index: number; level: Level }) {
  const r = 70;
  const c = 2 * Math.PI * r;
  return (
    <div className="relative grid size-[190px] place-items-center">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 160 160" className="size-full -rotate-90">
        <circle cx="80" cy="80" r={r} className="fill-none stroke-frost/10" strokeWidth="12" />
        <circle
          cx="80"
          cy="80"
          r={r}
          className={`fill-none ${LEVEL_STYLE[level].stroke} transition-all duration-700`}
          strokeWidth="12"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          strokeDasharray={c}
          strokeDashoffset={c * (1 - index / 100)}
        />
      </svg>
      <div className="absolute text-center">
        <div className={`font-display text-4xl font-bold ${LEVEL_STYLE[level].text}`}>
          {index.toFixed(2)}
        </div>
        <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">/ 100 index</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

interface RoadConditions {
  weather: Weather;
  volume: number;
  capacity: number;
  specialEvent: boolean;
  accident: boolean;
  construction: boolean;
}

const BASELINE: Record<Road, { volume: number; capacity: number }> = {
  "MG Road": { volume: 1800, capacity: 2000 },
  Whitefield: { volume: 1650, capacity: 2200 },
  "Electronic City": { volume: 2100, capacity: 2600 },
  Hebbal: { volume: 1500, capacity: 2400 },
  Indiranagar: { volume: 1400, capacity: 1800 },
};

function defaultConditions(): Record<Road, RoadConditions> {
  return ROADS.reduce(
    (acc, r) => {
      acc[r] = {
        weather: "Clear",
        volume: BASELINE[r].volume,
        capacity: BASELINE[r].capacity,
        specialEvent: false,
        accident: false,
        construction: false,
      };
      return acc;
    },
    {} as Record<Road, RoadConditions>,
  );
}

function Index() {
  const [road, setRoad] = useState<Road>("MG Road");
  const [hour, setHour] = useState(18);
  const [dayType, setDayType] = useState<DayType>("Weekday");
  const [conditions, setConditions] =
    useState<Record<Road, RoadConditions>>(defaultConditions);

  const current = conditions[road];
  const { weather, volume, capacity, specialEvent, accident, construction } = current;

  function updateRoad(patch: Partial<RoadConditions>) {
    setConditions((prev) => ({ ...prev, [road]: { ...prev[road], ...patch } }));
  }
  const setWeather = (v: Weather) => updateRoad({ weather: v });
  const setVolume = (v: number) => updateRoad({ volume: v });
  const setCapacity = (v: number) => updateRoad({ capacity: v });
  const setSpecialEvent = (v: boolean) => updateRoad({ specialEvent: v });
  const setAccident = (v: boolean) => updateRoad({ accident: v });
  const setConstruction = (v: boolean) => updateRoad({ construction: v });
  const resetThisRoad = () =>
    setConditions((prev) => ({ ...prev, [road]: defaultConditions()[road] }));
  const resetAllRoads = () => setConditions(defaultConditions());

  const input = { road, hour, dayType, ...current };

  const index = predictTraffic(input);
  const level = classifyTraffic(index);
  const confidence = calculateConfidence(road);
  const style = LEVEL_STYLE[level];
  const factors = breakdown(input);
  const ratio = vcRatio(volume, capacity);
  const analysis = useMemo(() => roadAnalysis(), []);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<"peak" | "roads">("peak");

  const hh = String(hour).padStart(2, "0");

  return (
    <div className="relative min-h-screen w-full overflow-hidden bg-ink font-body text-foreground">
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute -left-24 -top-40 size-[520px] rounded-full bg-pulse/25 blur-3xl" />
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute -right-20 top-24 size-[440px] rounded-full bg-flow/20 blur-3xl" />
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-1/3 size-[420px] rounded-full bg-jam/15 blur-3xl" />

      <div className="relative mx-auto max-w-6xl px-5 py-10 sm:px-6">
        <header className="mb-10 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-xl border border-frost/20 bg-frost/10 backdrop-blur-xl">
              <span className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-pulse">T</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="font-display text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight">
                TrafficGuard AI
              </h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                Traffic Congestion Prediction Agent · Bengaluru
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-frost/15 bg-frost/5 px-4 py-2 backdrop-blur-xl">
            <span className="size-2 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-flow" />
            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Model v2.4 · Live</span>
          </div>
        </header>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-12 gap-6">
          {/* ------- input form ------- */}
          <section className="col-span-12 lg:col-span-5">
            <div className="glass-panel rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7">
              <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <h2 className="font-display text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground">
                  Prediction Inputs · {road}
                </h2>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={resetThisRoad}
                  className="rounded-xl border border-frost/15 bg-frost/5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground hover:border-frost/30 hover:text-foreground"
                >
                  Reset this road
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-5">
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="road" className="mb-2 block text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    Road segment
                  </label>
                  <select
                    id="road"
                    value={road}
                    onChange={(e) => setRoad(e.target.value as Road)}
                    className="w-full cursor-pointer appearance-none rounded-xl border border-frost/15 bg-frost/5 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-pulse/50"
                  >
                    {ROADS.map((r, i) => (
                      <option key={r} value={r} className="bg-surface text-foreground">
                        {i + 1}. {r} (avg {historicalAverage(r).toFixed(2)})
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="hour"
                    className="mb-2 flex justify-between text-sm text-muted-foreground"
                  >
                    <span>Future hour (0–23)</span>
                    <span className="font-display font-semibold text-pulse">
                      {hh}:00{isPeakHour(hour) ? " · peak" : ""}
                    </span>
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="hour"
                    type="range"
                    min={0}
                    max={23}
                    value={hour}
                    onChange={(e) => setHour(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full accent-pulse"
                    aria-valuetext={`${hh}:00`}
                  />
                  <div className="mt-1 flex justify-between text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
                    <span>00:00</span>
                    <span>07–10 +20</span>
                    <span>17–20 +22</span>
                    <span>23:00</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <span className="mb-2 block text-sm text-muted-foreground">Day type</span>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                    {(["Weekday", "Weekend"] as DayType[]).map((d) => (
                      <button
                        key={d}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setDayType(d)}
                        aria-pressed={dayType === d}
                        className={
                          dayType === d
                            ? "rounded-xl border border-pulse/50 bg-pulse/20 px-2 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold"
                            : "rounded-xl border border-frost/15 bg-frost/5 px-2 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground hover:border-frost/30"
                        }
                      >
                        {d}
                        {d === "Weekend" ? " (−5)" : ""}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <span className="mb-2 block text-sm text-muted-foreground">Weather</span>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                    {WEATHERS.map((w) => (
                      <button
                        key={w.key}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setWeather(w.key)}
                        aria-pressed={weather === w.key}
                        className={
                          weather === w.key
                            ? "rounded-xl border border-pulse/50 bg-pulse/20 px-2 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold"
                            : "rounded-xl border border-frost/15 bg-frost/5 px-2 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground hover:border-frost/30"
                        }
                      >
                        <span className="mr-1">{w.icon}</span>
                        {w.key}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <label
                      htmlFor="volume"
                      className="mb-2 block text-sm text-muted-foreground"
                    >
                      Vehicle volume
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="volume"
                      type="number"
                      min={0}
                      value={volume}
                      onChange={(e) => setVolume(Math.max(0, Number(e.target.value)))}
                      className="w-full rounded-xl border border-frost/15 bg-frost/5 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-pulse/50"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label
                      htmlFor="capacity"
                      className="mb-2 block text-sm text-muted-foreground"
                    >
                      Road capacity
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="capacity"
                      type="number"
                      min={0}
                      value={capacity}
                      onChange={(e) => setCapacity(Math.max(0, Number(e.target.value)))}
                      className="w-full rounded-xl border border-frost/15 bg-frost/5 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-pulse/50"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="glass-chip rounded-2xl p-4">
                  <div className="mb-2 flex justify-between text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    <span>Volume / capacity ratio</span>
                    <span className="font-display font-semibold text-foreground">
                      {ratio.toFixed(1)}%
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-frost/10">
                    <div
                      className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all ${
                        ratio >= 80 ? "bg-jam" : ratio >= 60 ? "bg-watch" : "bg-flow"
                      }`}
                      style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, ratio)}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <Toggle
                    label="Special event nearby (+12)"
                    value={specialEvent}
                    onChange={setSpecialEvent}
                  />
                  <Toggle
                    label="Accident reported nearby (+20)"
                    value={accident}
                    onChange={setAccident}
                  />
                  <Toggle
                    label="Road construction / closure (+15)"
                    value={construction}
                    onChange={setConstruction}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* ------- results card ------- */}
          <section className="col-span-12 lg:col-span-7">
            <div className="glass-panel rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7">
              <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <h2 className="font-display text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground">
                  Traffic Prediction
                </h2>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span
                    className={`flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${style.chip}`}
                  >
                    <span className={`size-2 animate-pulse rounded-full ${style.dot}`} />
                    {level}
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className={`flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${
                      isPeakHour(hour)
                        ? "border-jam/25 bg-jam/15 text-jam"
                        : "border-frost/15 bg-frost/5 text-muted-foreground"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span
                      className={`size-2 rounded-full ${
                        isPeakHour(hour) ? "animate-pulse bg-jam" : "bg-frost/40"
                      }`}
                    />
                    PEAK TIME ALERT: {isPeakHour(hour) ? "Yes" : "No"}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div
                key={`${road}-${hour}-${weather}-${dayType}-${volume}-${capacity}-${specialEvent}-${accident}-${construction}`}
                className="animate-fade-rise"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-6 sm:flex-row">
                  <Gauge index={index} level={level} />
                  <div className="flex-1 space-y-3">
                    <div className="glass-chip rounded-2xl p-4">
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Congestion level</p>
                      <p className={`mt-1 font-display text-2xl font-semibold ${style.text}`}>
                        {level}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="glass-chip rounded-2xl p-4">
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Prediction confidence</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 font-display text-2xl font-semibold">
                        {confidence.toFixed(1)}%
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="glass-chip rounded-2xl p-4">
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        {road} · {hh}:00 · {dayType}
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-foreground">
                        {trafficBar(index)} {index.toFixed(1)} / 100
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* traffic bar */}
                <div className="mt-7">
                  <div className="mb-2 flex justify-between text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    <span>Free flow</span>
                    <span>Gridlock</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="h-3 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-frost/10">
                    <div
                      className="animate-bar-grow h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-flow via-watch to-jam"
                      style={{ width: `${index}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center text-[11px]">
                    {(["LOW", "MEDIUM", "HIGH"] as Level[]).map((l) => (
                      <div
                        key={l}
                        className={`rounded-xl border py-2 ${
                          level === l
                            ? LEVEL_STYLE[l].chip
                            : "border-frost/10 bg-frost/5 text-muted-foreground"
                        }`}
                      >
                        <span
                          className={`mx-auto mb-1 block size-2 rounded-full ${LEVEL_STYLE[l].dot}`}
                        />
                        {l.charAt(0) + l.slice(1).toLowerCase()}
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="glass-chip mt-4 rounded-2xl p-4">
                  <p className="mb-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
                    Recommendation
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed">{recommendation(level)}</p>
                </div>

                {/* breakdown */}
                <div className="glass-chip mt-4 rounded-2xl p-4">
                  <p className="mb-3 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
                    Factor breakdown
                  </p>
                  <ul className="space-y-1.5 text-sm">
                    {factors.map((f) => (
                      <li key={f.label} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                          {f.label}
                          {f.detail ? (
                            <span className="ml-1 text-xs opacity-70">({f.detail})</span>
                          ) : null}
                        </span>
                        <span
                          className={`font-display font-semibold ${
                            f.value > 0
                              ? "text-jam"
                              : f.value < 0
                                ? "text-flow"
                                : "text-muted-foreground"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {f.value > 0 ? "+" : ""}
                          {f.value.toFixed(2)}
                        </span>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                    <li className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-frost/10 pt-2">
                      <span className="text-sm">Traffic index (clamped 0–100)</span>
                      <span className={`font-display font-semibold ${style.text}`}>
                        {index.toFixed(2)}
                      </span>
                    </li>
                  </ul>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* ------- road network preview ------- */}
          <section className="col-span-12">
            <div className="glass-panel rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7">
              <div className="mb-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <h2 className="font-display text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground">
                  Road Network · conditions per segment
                </h2>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={resetAllRoads}
                  className="rounded-xl border border-frost/15 bg-frost/5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground hover:border-frost/30 hover:text-foreground"
                >
                  Reset all roads
                </button>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5">
                {ROADS.map((r) => {
                  const rc = conditions[r];
                  const val = predictTraffic({ road: r, hour, dayType, ...rc });
                  const l = classifyTraffic(val);
                  const active = r === road;
                  const chips: { label: string; cls: string }[] = [];
                  if (rc.weather === "Rain")
                    chips.push({ label: "☂ Rain", cls: "border-pulse/30 bg-pulse/15 text-pulse" });
                  if (rc.weather === "Cloudy")
                    chips.push({
                      label: "☁ Cloudy",
                      cls: "border-frost/20 bg-frost/10 text-muted-foreground",
                    });
                  if (rc.specialEvent)
                    chips.push({ label: "★ Event", cls: "border-watch/30 bg-watch/15 text-watch" });
                  if (rc.accident)
                    chips.push({ label: "⚠ Accident", cls: "border-jam/30 bg-jam/15 text-jam" });
                  if (rc.construction)
                    chips.push({ label: "⛏ Works", cls: "border-watch/30 bg-watch/15 text-watch" });
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={r}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setRoad(r)}
                      aria-pressed={active}
                      className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 text-left transition-colors ${
                        active
                          ? "border-pulse/50 bg-pulse/15"
                          : "border-frost/12 bg-frost/5 hover:border-frost/30"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
                        <span className="text-sm font-medium">{r}</span>
                        <span className={`size-2 rounded-full ${LEVEL_STYLE[l].dot}`} />
                      </div>
                      <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-frost/10">
                        <div
                          className={`h-full rounded-full ${LEVEL_STYLE[l].bar}`}
                          style={{ width: `${val}%` }}
                        />
                      </div>
                      <p className={`mt-2 font-display text-lg font-semibold ${LEVEL_STYLE[l].text}`}>
                        {val.toFixed(1)}
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
                        V/C {vcRatio(rc.volume, rc.capacity).toFixed(0)}%
                      </p>
                      <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                        {chips.length === 0 ? (
                          <span className="rounded-full border border-flow/25 bg-flow/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-flow">
                            ☀ Normal
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          chips.map((c) => (
                            <span
                              key={c.label}
                              className={`rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] ${c.cls}`}
                            >
                              {c.label}
                            </span>
                          ))
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* ------- peak hours + road analysis tabs ------- */}
          <section className="col-span-12">
            <div className="glass-panel rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7">
              <div className="mb-6 inline-flex gap-1 rounded-xl border border-frost/15 bg-frost/5 p-1">
                {(
                  [
                    { key: "peak", label: "Peak Hours" },
                    { key: "roads", label: "Road Analysis" },
                  ] as const
                ).map((t) => (
                  <button
                    key={t.key}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setTab(t.key)}
                    aria-pressed={tab === t.key}
                    className={`rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                      tab === t.key
                        ? "bg-pulse/25 text-foreground"
                        : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {t.label}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>

              {tab === "peak" ? (
                <div className="animate-fade-rise">
                  <p className="mb-5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    These periods have increased traffic according to the historical data.
                  </p>
                  <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    {PEAK_HOURS.map((p) => (
                      <div key={p.label} className="glass-chip rounded-2xl p-5">
                        <p className="font-display text-lg font-semibold">{p.label}</p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{p.window}</p>
                        <p className="mt-3 text-sm">
                          Time effect{" "}
                          <span className="font-display font-semibold text-jam">
                            +{p.from === 7 ? "20.00" : "22.00"}
                          </span>
                        </p>
                        {isPeakHour(hour) && hour >= p.from && hour <= p.to ? (
                          <span className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-jam/25 bg-jam/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-jam">
                            <span className="size-2 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-jam" />
                            Selected hour is inside this window
                          </span>
                        ) : null}
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="animate-fade-rise">
                  <p className="mb-5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    Average historical index per road segment.
                  </p>
                  <div className="space-y-4">
                    {analysis.map((a, i) => {
                      const l = classifyTraffic(a.average);
                      return (
                        <div key={a.road}>
                          <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
                            <span>
                              {i + 1}. {a.road}{" "}
                              <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                                ({a.samples} samples)
                              </span>
                            </span>
                            <span
                              className={`font-display font-semibold ${LEVEL_STYLE[l].text}`}
                            >
                              {a.average.toFixed(2)}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-frost/10">
                            <div
                              className={`animate-bar-grow h-full rounded-full ${LEVEL_STYLE[l].bar}`}
                              style={{ width: `${a.average}%` }}
                            />
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </section>
        </div>

        <p className="mt-8 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          TrafficGuard AI · predictions are illustrative, not live telemetry
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
# Routes

TanStack Start uses **file-based routing**. Every `.tsx` file in this directory
defines a route. Do **not** create `src/pages/`, `src/routes/_app/index.tsx`, or
`app/layout.tsx` — those are Next.js / Remix conventions. The only root layout
is `src/routes/__root.tsx`.

## Conventions

| File | URL |
| --- | --- |
| `index.tsx` | `/` |
| `about.tsx` | `/about` |
| `users/index.tsx` | `/users` |
| `users/$id.tsx` | `/users/:id` (dynamic — bare `$`, no curly braces) |
| `posts/{-$category}.tsx` | `/posts/:category?` (optional segment) |
| `files/$.tsx` | `/files/*` (splat — read via `_splat` param, never `*`) |
| `_layout.tsx` | layout route (renders children via `<Outlet />`) |
| `__root.tsx` | app shell — wraps every page; preserve `<Outlet />` |

`routeTree.gen.ts` is auto-generated. Don't edit it by hand.
import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createRouter } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { routeTree } from "./routeTree.gen";

export const getRouter = () => {
  const queryClient = new QueryClient();

  const router = createRouter({
    routeTree,
    context: { queryClient },
    scrollRestoration: true,
    defaultPreloadStaleTime: 0,
  });

  return router;
};
/* eslint-disable */

// @ts-nocheck

// noinspection JSUnusedGlobalSymbols

// This file was automatically generated by TanStack Router.
// You should NOT make any changes in this file as it will be overwritten.

import { Route as IndexRouteImport } from './routes/index'

const IndexRoute = IndexRouteImport.update({
  id: '/',
  path: '/',
  getParentRoute: () => rootRouteImport,
} as any)

export interface FileRoutesByFullPath {
  '/': typeof IndexRoute
}
export interface FileRoutesByTo {
  '/': typeof IndexRoute
}
export interface FileRoutesById {
  __root__: typeof rootRouteImport
  '/': typeof IndexRoute
}
export interface FileRouteTypes {
  fileRoutesByFullPath: FileRoutesByFullPath
  fullPaths: '/'
  fileRoutesByTo: FileRoutesByTo
  to: '/'
  id: '__root__' | '/'
  fileRoutesById: FileRoutesById
}
export interface RootRouteChildren {
  IndexRoute: typeof IndexRoute
}

declare module '@tanstack/react-router' {
  interface FileRoutesByPath {
    '/': {
      id: '/'
      path: '/'
      fullPath: '/'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof IndexRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
  }
}

const rootRouteChildren: RootRouteChildren = {
  IndexRoute: IndexRoute,
}
export const routeTree = rootRouteImport
  ._addFileChildren(rootRouteChildren)
  ._addFileTypes<FileRouteTypes>()

  interface Register {
    ssr: true

import "./lib/error-capture";

import { consumeLastCapturedError } from "./lib/error-capture";
import { renderErrorPage } from "./lib/error-page";

type ServerEntry = {
  fetch: (request: Request, env: unknown, ctx: unknown) => Promise<Response> | Response;
};

let serverEntryPromise: Promise<ServerEntry> | undefined;

async function getServerEntry(): Promise<ServerEntry> {
  if (!serverEntryPromise) {
    serverEntryPromise = import("@tanstack/react-start/server-entry").then(
      (m) => (m.default ?? m) as ServerEntry,
    );
  }
  return serverEntryPromise;
}

// h3 swallows in-handler throws into a normal 500 Response with body
// {"unhandled":true,"message":"HTTPError"} — try/catch alone never fires for those.
async function normalizeCatastrophicSsrResponse(response: Response): Promise<Response> {
  if (response.status < 500) return response;
  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  if (!contentType.includes("application/json")) return response;

  const body = await response.clone().text();
  if (!isH3SwallowedErrorBody(body)) return response;

  console.error(consumeLastCapturedError() ?? new Error(`h3 swallowed SSR error: ${body}`));
  return new Response(renderErrorPage(), {
    status: 500,
    headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" },
  });
}

function isH3SwallowedErrorBody(body: string): boolean {
  try {
    const payload = JSON.parse(body) as { unhandled?: unknown; message?: unknown };
    return payload.unhandled === true && payload.message === "HTTPError";
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export default {
  async fetch(request: Request, env: unknown, ctx: unknown) {
    try {
      const handler = await getServerEntry();
      const response = await handler.fetch(request, env, ctx);
      return await normalizeCatastrophicSsrResponse(response);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      return new Response(renderErrorPage(), {
        status: 500,
        headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" },
      });
    }
  },
};
import { createStart, createCsrfMiddleware, createMiddleware } from "@tanstack/react-start";

import { renderErrorPage } from "./lib/error-page";

const errorMiddleware = createMiddleware().server(async ({ next }) => {
  try {
    return await next();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error != null && typeof error === "object" && "statusCode" in error) {
      throw error;
    }
    console.error(error);
    return new Response(renderErrorPage(), {
      status: 500,
      headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" },
    });
  }
});

// Start installs this automatically when src/start.ts is absent; defining the
// file opts out, so re-add it explicitly to keep server functions protected
// from cross-site requests.
const csrfMiddleware = createCsrfMiddleware({
  filter: (ctx) => ctx.handlerType === "serverFn",
});

export const startInstance = createStart(() => ({
  requestMiddleware: [errorMiddleware, csrfMiddleware],
}));
